  (x : tuple.tuple_of (ssrnat.muln n d)

           Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__reals_Real
           Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R)))
  (fun i : fintype.ordinal n =>
   @tuple.mktuple d
     (lebesgue_stieltjes_measure.measurableTypeR

           RobustDefinitions.robust_points.u0} reals.Real.type Set
           Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__reals_Real

     (fun j : fintype.ordinal d =>
      @ListDef.nth

              RobustDefinitions.robust_points.u0} reals.Real.type Set

              Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R))

           (@fintype.nat_of_ord d j))
        (@tuple.tval (ssrnat.muln n d)
           (lebesgue_stieltjes_measure.measurableTypeR
              (@reverse_coercion@{reals.Real.type.u0
                 RobustDefinitions.robust_points.u0} reals.Real.type Set
                 Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__reals_Real
                 Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R))
           x)
        (@nmodule.Algebra.zero
           (reals.Real.Exports.reals_Real__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
              (@reverse_coercion@{reals.Real.type.u0
                 RobustDefinitions.robust_points.u0} reals.Real.type Set

RobustDefinitions.contaminated_weights =
fun (lambda : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R) (n N : nat)
  (w : forall _ : nat, Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R) 
  (s : nat) =>
match
  ssrnat.leq (S s) N
  return
    (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
       (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
          Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzRing))
with
| @true =>
    @rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
      (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
         Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzRing)
      (@nmodule.Algebra.add
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddMagma
            (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
               Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzRing))
         (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
            (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzRing.Exports.join_GRing_PzRing_between_Algebra_BaseZmodule_and_GRing_PzSemiRing
               Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzRing))
         (@nmodule.Algebra.opp
            Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__Algebra_BaseZmodule
            (@rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
               Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzSemiRing lambda
               (@divalg.GRing.inv
                  Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing
                  (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                        (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                           Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing))
                     (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                        (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                           Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing))
                     n)))))
      (w s)
| @false =>
    match
      @eqtype.eq_op ssrnat.Datatypes_nat__canonical__eqtype_Equality s N
      return
        (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.sort
           Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzSemiRing)
    with
    | @true =>
        @rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.mul
          Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzSemiRing lambda
          (@divalg.GRing.inv
             Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing
             (@nmodule.Algebra.natmul
                (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
                   (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                      Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing))
                (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.one
                   (divalg.GRing.UnitRing.Exports.GRing_UnitRing__to__GRing_PzSemiRing
                      Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_UnitRing))
                n))
    | @false =>
        @nmodule.Algebra.zero
          (rings_modules_and_algebras.GRing.PzSemiRing.Exports.GRing_PzSemiRing__to__Algebra_BaseAddUMagma
             Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__GRing_PzSemiRing)

     Definition robust_statement : Prop.
